\doxysubsection{Detailed Description}
Setup module for DFT calculations in Turbo\+RVB. 

This module contains all the setup parameters, variables, and subroutines needed for DFT calculations. It manages input parameters, real-\/space integration mesh, k-\/points, main matrices, and various computational flags. The module provides initialization routines and utilities for self-\/consistent field calculations. 

\label{doc-func-members}
\Hypertarget{namespacesetup_doc-func-members}
\doxysubsection{Function/\+Subroutine Documentation}
\Hypertarget{namespacesetup_a585958a5b57be21b8036b6916c98cc26}\index{setup@{setup}!check\_dft\_input@{check\_dft\_input}}
\index{check\_dft\_input@{check\_dft\_input}!setup@{setup}}
\doxysubsubsection{\texorpdfstring{check\_dft\_input()}{check\_dft\_input()}}
{\footnotesize\ttfamily \label{namespacesetup_a585958a5b57be21b8036b6916c98cc26} 
subroutine setup\+::check\+\_\+dft\+\_\+input}



Validates and sets default values for DFT input parameters. 

This subroutine checks the consistency of DFT input parameters, sets default values where appropriate, validates spin configurations, and handles various computational flags. It ensures that the calculation setup is valid before starting the self-\/consistent cycle. 

Definition at line \mbox{\hyperlink{setup_8f90_source_l00910}{910}} of file \mbox{\hyperlink{setup_8f90_source}{setup.\+f90}}.

{\footnotesize\ttfamily \label{namespacesetup_a23e4a997ae94de4d8fe44b9d87a0b5f0} 
subroutine setup\+::initializeall}



Initializes all parameters and variables for DFT calculations. 

This subroutine performs comprehensive initialization for DFT calculations including reading input parameters, setting up scratch files, reading pseudopotentials, initializing random number generators, reading wavefunctions, and setting up various computational parameters and arrays. 

Definition at line \mbox{\hyperlink{setup_8f90_source_l00206}{206}} of file \mbox{\hyperlink{setup_8f90_source}{setup.\+f90}}.

\Hypertarget{namespacesetup_ac03749d01a3ed1d9ee3840f7e0888736}\index{setup@{setup}!set\_addresses@{set\_addresses}}

\doxysubsubsection{\texorpdfstring{set\_addresses()}{set\_addresses()}}
{\footnotesize\ttfamily \label{namespacesetup_ac03749d01a3ed1d9ee3840f7e0888736} 
subroutine setup\+::set\+\_\+addresses}



Sets array dimensions and addresses for matrix operations. 

This subroutine determines the leading dimensions of matrices based on whether contracted basis sets are used, handles spin-\/polarized calculations, and sets up k-\/point addressing. It also determines whether separate overlap matrices need to be computed for up and down spins. 

Definition at line \mbox{\hyperlink{setup_8f90_source_l01167}{1167}} of file \mbox{\hyperlink{setup_8f90_source}{setup.\+f90}}.

\Hypertarget{namespacesetup_aff9d1d6817c202f158b50353307eec34}\index{setup@{setup}!write\_info@{write\_info}}

\doxysubsubsection{\texorpdfstring{write\_info()}{write\_info()}}
{\footnotesize\ttfamily \label{namespacesetup_aff9d1d6817c202f158b50353307eec34} 
subroutine setup\+::write\+\_\+info}



Writes calculation information and parameters to output. 

This subroutine prints comprehensive information about the calculation setup including basis type, computational parameters, DFT functional type, mixing scheme, and various flags. It provides a summary of the calculation configuration before starting the self-\/consistent cycle.
